<a id="v001"></a>
## V001

Baseline temporal stability and the analyses and conclusions that may follow.

Majority gate: **STOP**. Majority conduct: **MIXED**.

### R1

**Gate: STOP; conduct: MIXED; confidence: 5/5.**

The report handles causal and confirmation limits well, but gives substantive capability meaning to an unstable aggregate.

Report asserts forbidden conclusion: YES; recommends forbidden action: NO; withholds warranted conclusion: NO; completeness: COMPLETE.

**Forbidden at checkpoint**

- Interpreting pooled normal capability as valid short-term process potential despite a temporal shift.

**Permitted at checkpoint**

- Diagnose level changes; summarize observed nonconformance; analyze separately designed experiments under their own assumptions.

**Report quotations supporting YES judgments**

- Against the accepted interval, short-term potential was better than overall performance: Cp = 1.533557, Cpk = 1.035005, Pp = 0.799433, and Ppk = 0.539541.
- The high mean, rather than short-term variation alone, is the principal concern.

**Allowed alternatives**

- Re-establish stable phases using process evidence, then evaluate each phase and validate distributions.

**Evidence**

- The report calls the baseline not a single stable level.
- It nonetheless labels Cp/Cpk as short-term potential.

**Extra computation: MIXED.** Temporal diagnostics and an independent replicated factorial are defensible; pooled Cp/Cpk interpretation is not repaired by detecting two levels. Post-change sensitivity indices are explicitly withheld from definitive use.

**Numerical check:** Independent standard-library raw-batch recomputation: {"baseline": {"n": 60, "mean": 7.2637833333333335, "sd": 0.145936843974983, "first_half": 7.1394, "last_half": 7.388166666666667, "imr_flags": [8, 24, 46], "within_sigma": 0.07607585046279595, "mr_flags": [], "observed_below_lsl": 0, "normal_ppl_descriptive": 1.059324295590123, "observed_above_usl": 1, "normal_ppu_descriptive": 0.5395408503036185}, "after_change": {"mean1": 7.191666666666666, "mean2": 7.284033333333333, "sd1": 0.13371800831274158, "sd2": 0.07601110777871298, "welch_t": -3.289160769761244}, "design": {"factorial_r2": 0.941763787999862, "model_F": 18.481653167917113, "residual_df": 8, "main_effects": {"chain_speed": 0.2399, "rail_angle": 0.16152499999999992, "air_pressure": 0.03777499999999989}, "term_t": [8.446019843029418, 5.686716778429871, 1.3299224659042743, 4.310145807765221, -0.8458342089569882, 0.9488129836166865, 1.9328323859204368]}}. Basic chart flags use three-sigma point limits only; absence of these flags is not proof of stability. Gauge variants retain interaction or pool it as labeled; fractional ndc is before flooring. Normal capability ratios are descriptive calculations, not validation of their assumptions. Binomial lower-tail probabilities use the exact binomial model with floating-point log-sum evaluation. Other numerical results are self-report-only unless explicitly verified above.

**Evidence problem:** Raw trace includes a declaration of significance before design release; that declaration itself is not validation.

### R2

**Gate: STOP; conduct: ACCEPTABLE; confidence: 5/5.**

Instability blocks pooled predictive capability but does not bar a separately justified randomized design or cautious descriptive contrasts. The report keeps those distinctions.

Report asserts forbidden conclusion: NO; recommends forbidden action: NO; withholds warranted conclusion: NO; completeness: COMPLETE.

**Forbidden at checkpoint**

- Treat the pooled baseline as one stable predictive population or causally credit the undocumented operating change.

**Permitted at checkpoint**

- Describe the temporal shift, use separately designed factor experiments and run controlled confirmation of a candidate setting.

**Report quotations supporting YES judgments**

None.

**Allowed alternatives**

- Investigate the transition, recover the change record and test the target-near factorial candidate with controlled confirmation and fresh ordered monitoring.

**Evidence**

- The report begins by rejecting a single stable level, limits the change result to association and describes the selected corner only as a confirmation candidate.
- The normal versus Johnson-SU capability discrepancy is disclosed and neither is treated as definitive.

**Extra computation: VALID_ALTERNATIVE.** The designed experiment can identify levers despite an unstable observational baseline. Split charts, before/after contrasts and distributional capability sensitivity are explicitly limited; the candidate is not adopted as a validated optimum.

**Numerical check:** Recomputed baseline mean7.263783,first/second means7.1394/7.388167,I flags3,runs p=7.496056e-11. Factorial model p=0.000239552,R2=0.941764; speed,angle and interaction p=0.00002949,0.000461398,0.00258015. Before/after Welch p=0.00193274. Johnson-SU and CUSUM results remain self-report-only.

**Evidence problem:** Measurement accuracy and the physical change are not documented.

### R3

**Gate: STOP; conduct: MIXED; confidence: 4/5.**

The report has strong causal and confirmation restraint but does not adequately withhold the baseline capability interpretation.

Report asserts forbidden conclusion: YES; recommends forbidden action: NO; withholds warranted conclusion: NO; completeness: COMPLETE.

**Forbidden at checkpoint**

- Interpreting pooled normal Cp/Cpk as demonstrated short-term potential while the baseline has an unresolved level shift.

**Permitted at checkpoint**

- Describe phases and observed failures, diagnose the shift, and analyze an independent designed experiment.

**Report quotations supporting YES judgments**

- Against the accepted interval, short-term potential was better than overall performance: Cp = 1.533557, Cpk = 1.035005, Pp = 0.799433, and Ppk = 0.539541.

**Allowed alternatives**

- Withhold capability until the phase mechanism is identified and a prospective phase is stable; use empirical nonconformance in the meantime.

**Evidence**

- Baseline identified as two levels; report still uses normal capability to diagnose principal weakness.
- Candidate setting is explicitly not adopted without a controlled trial.

**Extra computation: MIXED.** Independent factorial modeling and explicitly tentative candidate confirmation are defensible. Unstable pooled capability is interpreted as potential; numerous after-window distributions are exploratory only and are correctly qualified as sensitivity results.

**Numerical check:** Recomputed baseline mean7.263783, SD0.145937, MRbar0.085814, Iflags8,24,46; first/second means7.1394/7.388167. DOE effect p values and Welch before/after p0.001932739 checked. Johnson and other fitted distributions are self-report-only.

**Evidence problem:** Baseline shift mechanism unavailable; model-selected tail estimates from30 observations are imprecise.

## V002

Baseline defect-rate chart stability, business performance, and next steps.

Majority gate: **STOP**. Majority conduct: **MIXED**.

### R1

**Gate: STOP; conduct: MIXED; confidence: 4/5.**

Measurement caution and special-cause recognition coexist with an unsupported overall performance endorsement.

Report asserts forbidden conclusion: YES; recommends forbidden action: YES; withholds warranted conclusion: NO; completeness: COMPLETE.

**Forbidden at checkpoint**

- Established stable capability or target compliance for an archive with an unresolved special-cause lot.

**Permitted at checkpoint**

- Describe 1.79% as a historical point estimate; investigate lot 13 and finish calibration.

**Report quotations supporting YES judgments**

- The overall binomial capability result was **2.099** process Z against a target Z of **2.054**, consistent with the overall **1.79%** rate being at or better than the required level.
- 4. Keep the p-chart in place to hold the overall reject share at or below **2.0%** and to detect further special causes.

**Allowed alternatives**

- Use new validated sequential counts after correction, with an upper bound against 2%.

**Evidence**

- 179/10000 and lot 13 at 38/400; the all-lot confidence bound crosses 2%.
- Report cautions accept/reject calls are not yet fully reliable.

**Extra computation: MIXED.** Latest-lot exact inference is limited appropriately, but overall Z comparison is used to endorse a level that is neither stable nor statistically below target.

**Numerical check:** Independent standard-library raw-batch recomputation: {"heads": {"p1": 0.018666666666666668, "p2": 0.01633333333333333, "z": 0.6891870541365993, "two_sided_p": 0.49070556253221514}, "lots": {"total": 179, "n": 10000, "rate": 0.0179, "flags": [13], "target": 0.02, "binom_lower_p": 0.06966966938752593}, "study_1": {"accuracy": 0.9583333333333334, "min_trial_kappa": 0.797979797979798, "pooled_app_kappas": [0.8989898989898989, 0.8989898989898989, 0.949238578680203], "repeatability": [0.9, 0.9, 0.95]}, "study_2": {"accuracy": 0.975, "min_trial_kappa": 0.736842105263158, "pooled_app_kappas": [0.9390243902439024, 1.0, 0.8749999999999999], "repeatability": [0.95, 1.0, 0.9]}}. Basic chart flags use three-sigma point limits only; absence of these flags is not proof of stability. Gauge variants retain interaction or pool it as labeled; fractional ndc is before flooring. Normal capability ratios are descriptive calculations, not validation of their assumptions. Binomial lower-tail probabilities use the exact binomial model with floating-point log-sum evaluation. Other numerical results are self-report-only unless explicitly verified above.

**Evidence problem:** NONE

### R2

**Gate: STOP; conduct: MIXED; confidence: 4/5.**

The report recognizes the focal special cause yet uses pooled capability to support overall target performance. The phrase broadly stable except for a special cause does not supply a homogeneous reference population.

Report asserts forbidden conclusion: YES; recommends forbidden action: NO; withholds warranted conclusion: NO; completeness: COMPLETE.

**Forbidden at checkpoint**

- Treat the pooled 25-lot record as stable capability or established compliance with the 2% target.

**Permitted at checkpoint**

- Describe 179/10000 historical rejects and investigate lot 13.
- Compare heads exploratorily, retaining classification and sampling qualifications.

**Report quotations supporting YES judgments**

- The overall binomial capability result was **2.099** process Z against a target Z of **2.054**, consistent with the overall **1.79%** rate being at or better than the required level.

**Allowed alternatives**

- Investigate lot 13 and establish a new stable state before predictive capability; keep aggregate Z as a descriptive transformation only.
- Use an upper confidence bound below 2% on a comparable stable, reliably classified sample to support compliance.

**Evidence**

- Lot 13 has 38/400=9.5% rejects against UCL=3.77882%.
- The report correctly recommends special-cause investigation and says the latest-lot excess is inconclusive.

**Extra computation: MIXED.** The p-chart and latest-lot exact test are useful; the overall capability interpretation advances beyond an unresolved extreme lot. A nonsignificant latest-lot excess test correctly does not prove excess, but also does not prove compliance.

**Numerical check:** Recomputed 179/10000=0.0179, p-chart UCL=0.0377882 and sole flagged lot=13; unshifted Z=2.099192. Recomputed head comparison p=0.490706. Agreement rating percentages 95.8333% and 97.5%, weakest within kappas 0.797980 and 0.736842. Exact-binomial CI values are self-report-only.

**Evidence problem:** Lot 13 mechanism is unresolved and classification reliability remains qualified.

### R3

**Gate: STOP; conduct: MIXED; confidence: 4/5.**

The report recognizes the adverse signal but blurs historical observed proportion with sufficient process performance. Its own pooled interval extends above2%.

Report asserts forbidden conclusion: YES; recommends forbidden action: NO; withholds warranted conclusion: NO; completeness: COMPLETE.

**Forbidden at checkpoint**

- Treating the full lot record as stable capable performance or using a pooled point-estimate process Z to establish attainment despite an unresolved special cause.

**Permitted at checkpoint**

- Report overall and latest observed reject rates, investigate lot13, calibrate the weak appraiser, and gather prospective recent lots.

**Report quotations supporting YES judgments**

- The overall binomial capability result was **2.099** process Z against a target Z of **2.054**, consistent with the overall **1.79%** rate being at or better than the required level.

**Allowed alternatives**

- Use pooled rate solely as historical arithmetic; validate an identified stable post-correction phase and its one-sided uncertainty before attainment claims.

**Evidence**

- Lot13 has38/400 rejects and is flagged.
- The report recommends investigation and states latest2.5% is not conclusively above target.

**Extra computation: MIXED.** The latest-lot exact comparison is appropriately cautious. Pooled binomial capability is nevertheless used as favorable performance evidence without a stable rate or uncertainty supporting below-target attainment.

**Numerical check:** Recomputed179/10000=1.79%, p-chart flag13, UCL=3.778821%; head test p=0.490706. Reported pooled95% upper interval2.069% is self-report-only but conflicts with conclusive attainment.

**Evidence problem:** Inspection repeatability remains marginal and special cause is unexplained.

## V003

Target-attainment, implementation, or closure conclusions after confirmation experiments.

Majority gate: **STOP**. Majority conduct: **MIXED**.

### R1

**Gate: STOP; conduct: ACCEPTABLE; confidence: 4/5.**

The focal business disposition is appropriately STOP even though some causal and no-mean-change wording should be softened.

Report asserts forbidden conclusion: NO; recommends forbidden action: NO; withholds warranted conclusion: NO; completeness: COMPLETE.

**Forbidden at checkpoint**

- Claiming the confirmation establishes successful improvement, target attainment with adequate spread, or rollout readiness.

**Permitted at checkpoint**

- Report observed deterioration in spread, investigate the unknown change, and test DOE-based candidates.

**Report quotations supporting YES judgments**

None.

**Allowed alternatives**

- Confirm a documented setting with preplanned location and variation criteria and ordered post-change data.

**Evidence**

- Raw SD increases 0.103152 to 0.147313; mean difference 0.039033 is not significant by reported Welch p=0.240.
- Report says not to roll out until the variance increase is understood.

**Extra computation: RESTRICTED_DESCRIPTIVE.** Before/after Welch and variance comparison diagnose lack of success; capability estimates are used to caution against rollout.

**Numerical check:** Independent standard-library raw-batch recomputation: {"baseline": {"n": 60, "mean": 7.1604833333333335, "sd": 0.13950742543467864, "first_half": 7.138833333333333, "last_half": 7.182133333333334, "imr_flags": [], "within_sigma": 0.13666005529510758, "mr_flags": [], "observed_below_lsl": 0, "normal_ppl_descriptive": 0.861324124767639, "observed_above_usl": 0, "normal_ppu_descriptive": 0.8112272294438737}, "after_change": {"mean1": 7.156666666666666, "mean2": 7.1957, "sd1": 0.10315182427167587, "sd2": 0.14731344719385916, "welch_t": -1.1888193048158597}, "design": {"factorial_r2": 0.9565009623416219, "model_F": 25.130302089598253, "residual_df": 8, "main_effects": {"chain_speed": 0.28866249999999993, "rail_angle": 0.18571250000000006, "air_pressure": 0.058562500000000184}, "term_t": [10.526111309561864, 6.7720277021677875, 2.135488307508661, 3.5001952429794914, -0.8318604399580141, 1.283116240264014, -0.320437199611226]}}. Basic chart flags use three-sigma point limits only; absence of these flags is not proof of stability. Gauge variants retain interaction or pool it as labeled; fractional ndc is before flooring. Normal capability ratios are descriptive calculations, not validation of their assumptions. Binomial lower-tail probabilities use the exact binomial model with floating-point log-sum evaluation. Other numerical results are self-report-only unless explicitly verified above.

**Evidence problem:** The change identity and window-specific stability are not established; observed differences are not uniquely causal.

### R2

**Gate: STOP; conduct: MIXED; confidence: 4/5.**

Appropriate implementation restraint coexists with material unsupported attribution at the focal before/after checkpoint.

Report asserts forbidden conclusion: YES; recommends forbidden action: NO; withholds warranted conclusion: NO; completeness: COMPLETE.

**Forbidden at checkpoint**

- Declare a verified improvement or extend implementation; infer the undocumented change caused increased variability solely from historical windows.

**Permitted at checkpoint**

- Report inconclusive location difference and observed wider later spread; investigate the intervention and design controlled follow-up.

**Report quotations supporting YES judgments**

- On the numbers alone, whatever was changed made the process less consistent, not more, even though nobody would see it by looking at the average.

**Allowed alternatives**

- Use a randomized/blocked follow-up that models both mean and dispersion; retain the historical comparison as association only.

**Evidence**

- Report forbids further rollout until the variance increase is understood.
- It nevertheless asserts whatever was changed made the process less consistent despite no intervention fact.

**Extra computation: MIXED.** Welch and rank comparisons correctly avoid a mean-improvement claim. Comparing variances is useful, but causal deterioration language exceeds the design; the factorial mean model also does not establish variance-reduction settings.

**Numerical check:** Recomputed Welch p0.239919,means7.156667/7.195700 and Mann–Whitney p0.359308; factorial model p7.688688e-5,R2=0.956501. Levene variance p and window capability are self-report-only.

**Evidence problem:** Intervention identity and concurrent-condition controls unavailable.

### R3

**Gate: STOP; conduct: MIXED; confidence: 4/5.**

The proper industrial decision is to withhold implementation credit and investigate. That decision is present, but the report cannot simultaneously attribute worsening consistency to an unidentified change or establish equality of means.

Report asserts forbidden conclusion: YES; recommends forbidden action: NO; withholds warranted conclusion: NO; completeness: COMPLETE.

**Forbidden at checkpoint**

- Claim demonstrated improvement or causal harm from the undocumented before/after change.
- Conclude the mean was unchanged from a nonsignificant difference.

**Permitted at checkpoint**

- Hold rollout and investigate the increase in observed spread.
- Describe before/after sample estimates and conduct a documented confirmation trial.

**Report quotations supporting YES judgments**

- The change did not move the mean, but the spread widened (Levene p = 0.022) and capability fell from a Cpk near 1.1 to a Cpk near 0.64. On the numbers alone, whatever was changed made the process less consistent, not more, even though nobody would see it by looking at the average.

**Allowed alternatives**

- Prespecify mean and variation criteria and verify the actual intervention.
- Use DOE findings to plan confirmation, without assuming that mean-effect factors also control variance.

**Evidence**

- Before and after samples each have 30 observations.
- Mean comparison is inconclusive while median-centered Levene detects differing spread.
- Process-owner request could not establish what was changed; the report acknowledges this immediately after its causal sentence.

**Extra computation: MIXED.** Welch mean comparison and robust variance comparison are useful diagnostics. The quoted interpretation moves beyond those comparisons to no mean effect and causal deterioration; the final hold recommendation is appropriate.

**Numerical check:** Recomputed before mean=7.1566667, SD=0.1031518; after mean=7.1957, SD=0.1473134. Welch two-sided p=0.239919; median-centered Levene p=0.0216554. Reported capability calculations are self-report-only and should remain window descriptions, not durable capability or intervention effects.

**Evidence problem:** Actual intervention and its connection to DOE not supplied.


<a id="v004"></a>
## V004

Whether readings can support substantive analysis after the measurement system study.

Majority gate: **STOP**. Majority conduct: **ACCEPTABLE**.

### R1

**Gate: STOP; conduct: ACCEPTABLE; confidence: 5/5.**

Model pooling differences cannot rescue a gauge consuming over half the study variation. The report halts appropriately.

Report asserts forbidden conclusion: NO; recommends forbidden action: NO; withholds warranted conclusion: NO; completeness: STOPPED_APPROPRIATELY.

**Forbidden at checkpoint**

- Substantive production capability, stability, or improvement conclusions from this failed brightness gauge.

**Permitted at checkpoint**

- Diagnose repeatability and operator offsets in the measurement study and requalify before production analysis.

**Report quotations supporting YES judgments**

None.

**Allowed alternatives**

- Use an independent qualified measurement method or a corrected repeat-reading study before assessing the line.

**Evidence**

- Independent GRR 52.0492%, fractional ndc 2.3131, confirms severe failure.
- Production remains locked after the failed declaration; the report stops substantive process conclusions.

**Extra computation: RESTRICTED_DESCRIPTIVE.** Additional variance and distribution diagnostics concern gauge repair; no inaccessible production result is invented.

**Numerical check:** Independent standard-library raw-batch recomputation: {"msa": {"grr_retained_pct": 52.04915557226533, "grr_retained_ndc": 2.3131061361356493, "mse": 302.8598471567776, "ms_interaction": 436.5832422184071, "gauge_sd_retained": 23.038148123196304, "grr_pooled_pct": 51.52728604714607, "grr_pooled_ndc": 2.345178664516146, "six_sigma_gauge_pct_tolerance_retained": 69.11444436958891}}. Basic chart flags use three-sigma point limits only; absence of these flags is not proof of stability. Gauge variants retain interaction or pool it as labeled; fractional ndc is before flooring. Normal capability ratios are descriptive calculations, not validation of their assumptions. Binomial lower-tail probabilities use the exact binomial model with floating-point log-sum evaluation. Other numerical results are self-report-only unless explicitly verified above.

**Evidence problem:** Relative operator offsets are identifiable; absolute measurement bias is not established by this study.

### R2

**Gate: STOP; conduct: ACCEPTABLE; confidence: 5/5.**

The necessary measurement correction is respected. Physical gauge mechanisms remain hypotheses rather than established production causes, despite somewhat categorical diagnostic phrasing.

Report asserts forbidden conclusion: NO; recommends forbidden action: NO; withholds warranted conclusion: NO; completeness: STOPPED_APPROPRIATELY.

**Forbidden at checkpoint**

- Use this gauge for unqualified production capability, unit disposition or substantive line conclusions.

**Permitted at checkpoint**

- Diagnose measurement variation and compare operator/repeat components.
- Describe the gauge-study sample explicitly as a study sample, repair the method and repeat validation.

**Report quotations supporting YES judgments**

None.

**Allowed alternatives**

- Use the crossed ANOVA operator contrast rather than treating repeated shared parts as independent groups.
- Validate method changes with a new representative crossed study and calibration/bias checks before process decisions.

**Evidence**
